## 1 November 2026 — goal links and cross-household activity reads

The local campaign branch includes two bounded R04 privacy corrections.
`d700f6d4` passes the authenticated actor into the goal-linked transaction
query. It applies the existing account and transaction visibility rules in SQL.
The owner can see their private source. Other members see the shared control
and get the same empty response for a private-only goal as for an empty goal.
The author target passed 90 tests in four files and retained zero goals, links,
transactions, accounts and other connections at postflight.

The first combined full server execution at `753b0d58` passed 3,662 of 3,664
tests. Two older tests expected authentication and configuration behavior
superseded by C2. `72b68122` tests the generic public verification response
during cooldown, unchanged credentials and delivery count, and rotation when
eligible. `9de46487` supplies the required public app origin in the TLS fixture
and checks the specific variable rejected for each insecure override.
The existing production controls were exercised by the updated tests.
All 22 focused tests and the full 272-file server suite passed at `9de46487`
(3,664 tests in 493.43s). The original failing run remains in the
capital-goals-integrate-nov01 worktree under `.local/goal-integrated-nov01a/`.
The regression worktree retains `.local/server-regression-nov01a/`, with
the full passing run and the marked target postflight.

The next commit `be5c0fe8` scopes goal activity reads to the goal household
and checks current readable membership on both SQL reads. Foreign and missing
goals return 404. A valid empty goal returns an empty array. Revocation between
lookups suppresses the activity rows. Five suites passed all 98 focused tests
on this commit. The marked target `goalacts01a` retained zero goals, activity
rows, links and other connections. Its author worktree retains the evidence in
`.local/goal-activity-nov01a/`. Astra checked source, execution receipts and
target readbacks before both local fast-forwards.

Within a household, activity balance snapshots, metadata and goal progress
can still reveal amounts derived from private sources. The existing activity
schema lacks reliable source attribution for several historical event types.
That response contract and full FIN-01 acceptance remain open.

## 1 November 2026 — report settings integrated and test cluster stopped

Local canonical source `aecdf7f4e91d12fcf344a9eef7e82979fb66c2d8` includes
the 41-path report port with its follow-up fixes. Report customization, reset,
onboarding and the dashboard now use the reviewed preference writers. The
report dialogs wait for an acknowledgement matching the submitted change.
The server writer and client reset validator share the same default layout.

Four regression cases failed on the preserved `414b0376` test candidate.
A delayed GET could undo an acknowledged PATCH or reset, and reset accepted
an unchanged custom layout or empty maps. At `54b2e505` all 13 cases and
controls passed. The 18 affected client files passed 166 tests.

Both original Chromium journeys failed on `8eef2084`. Widget cards covered
the onboarding footer, intercepting clicks on Save and Skip. The repaired
dialog keeps the footer outside a bounded scroll area and wraps preset text
on narrow screens. Both ordinary-click desktop journeys passed on
`65b699ac`. The browser log also recorded five failed audit inserts despite
successful HTTP writes. A PostgreSQL regression at `a333976a` reproduced
a successful PATCH with zero new audit rows. Both report routes had passed
a non-UUID string as the household ID for personal settings.

At `aecdf7f4`, PATCH and reset each produced a distinct audit row with the
signed-in user as actor and entity and a null household ID. Both desktop
Chromium journeys and a 320-by-740 Chromium setup/customize/reset journey
passed. They verify a fresh login, separate-user state, saved layout and
failed-save retry. The focused audit and browser logs contain no audit-write
errors. Repository check and build passed. The full protected server suite
passed 3,731 tests in 277 files. The full client suite passed 1,824 tests in
214 files at `65b699ac`, whose client tree is identical to the final commit.
Astra reviewed source, failures, repaired runs and postflight before the
local fast-forward. Full report acceptance still requires separate work on
chart-array conflicts and alignment between the widget registry and defaults.

Raw evidence is in the capital-report-integrate-nov01 worktree under
`.local/report-rehearsal-nov01a/` and `.local/report-current-nov01a/`, and
the capital-report-audit-nov01 worktree under `.local/audit-final-nov01a/`.
The historical report cache and audit test worktrees retain their RED runs.
The final marked target had zero other connections after fixture cleanup.
Coordinator checked the PostgreSQL 18 data directory, postmaster PID/start
identity, loopback port 55432 and zero other clients, then stopped the owned
cluster. Shutdown and the original startup handle both exited successfully.
Logs are in the canonical worktree under `.local/runtime-retry-nov01a/`.

The passing full suite also logged audit omissions on three other real
routes. Budget recalculate and clear use a bulk string in a UUID audit
column (BGR-04). Default-tag creation uses a non-UUID entity ID. Preserve
these as open R08 NEW-32/R04 work. The governing financial requirement
includes atomic audit or outbox recording and a valid bulk identity with
batch metadata. Passing HTTP responses do not establish those guarantees.
Other full-suite audit warnings were traced to mock fixtures and have a
different evidentiary scope.

### Next R04 work after the report checkpoint

The separate design commit `af3da9a6` in capital-r04-goal-progress-contract is
unaccepted. Independent review found that proving a progress value would not
authorize exposing every account reference or assignment balance on the DTO.
Legacy unlink and initial-balance history cannot prove a complete source chain.
Holdings-mode progress, milestone-modal callers and pay-down currency also need
explicit treatment. A redacted response is permitted by the foundation spec.
The proposed shape still needs revision before implementation.

The next bounded implementation is the reverse transaction-to-goals read,
`GET /api/transactions/:id/goals`. Its source transaction, account and linked
goal can each be checked without settling historical progress. The assigned
worktree is capital-r04-transaction-goals on codex/r04-transaction-goals from
`78265703`. Reconcile that author before any duplicate work. The test cluster
was stopped after the accepted report run. The existing owner grant permits
coordinated protected test work on the owned loopback target.

## 1 November 2026 — reverse transaction goal links integrated locally

The transaction editor's `GET /api/transactions/:id/goals` now checks the
actor's membership and visibility of both the transaction and its account.
The joined goal must belong to the same household. A visible unlinked
transaction returns an empty array. Missing and invisible transactions
return the same 404, and a malformed ID returns 400. The positive controls
preserve link IDs, amount and currency, ordering and the goal summary.

On the old campaign source `5e1b6daf`, ten of eleven signed-in HTTP tests
failed. The focused spec's Git blob `4f3d0b172d36e279cac8d915ea785889c7698bf0`
matches the test-first commit `5307903b`. Author `5d3d6090` and combined
commit `d99d8bb141d0d79095115b994efac460cde3972e` both passed all 84 tests
across four server suites on separate marked PostgreSQL targets. The combined
commit passed all 19 client hook tests, repository checks and build. Astra
reviewed the code, the original failures and the passing execution records
before the local fast-forward. This increment had no interactive editor run.

Evidence is in capital-reverse-baseline and capital-r04-transaction-goals
under `.local/reverse-goals-nov01a/`, and capital-reverse-integrate under
`.local/reverse-integrated-nov01a/`. The three marked targets retained no
transactions, transaction-goal links, goals or other connections. The
coordinator verified PID 16452, its start identity, data directory, loopback
port 55432 and zero other clients before shutting down the owned test cluster.
The startup and shutdown receipts are in `.local/reverse-goals-runtime-nov01a/`.

### R04 snapshot capture migration reservation

### November 2 — owner FX ruling and the provider validity repair

For a weekly recap transaction with a trusted provider quote and no expiry, the owner authorized carrying the rate for at most three calendar days after its quoted date. Day +3 is included. Day +4 is expired. This ruling is for recap transaction flows. It does not establish carry-forward rules for account balance valuations, overrides or arbitrary global manual rows.

Astra held the previous digest candidate for an uncategorized expense converted with a rate years out of date. Cases exercised concurrent identical refreshes, conflicting replay, compatibility precision, unchanged manual rows, fallback observation date, the three-day boundary, expired rates and recipient-visible totals. Wider reporting, approved household overrides, durable delivery and valuation requirements remain open.
